<div className="bible-chapter" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-lang="en" data-translation="nmv" data-translation-id="eng_nmv" data-translation-label="NMV" data-book="NUM" data-book-slug="numbers" data-book-name="Numbers" data-chapter="1" data-verses="1288">

<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; in the wilderness of Sinai, in the tabernacle of the congregation, on the first &#91;day&#93; of the second month, in the second year after they were come out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Take you2f the sum of all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, after their families, by the house of their fathers, with the number of &#91;their&#93; names, every male by their polls;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> From twenty years old and upward, all that are able to go forth to war in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: youi and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall number them by their armies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And with you there shall be a man of every tribe; every one head of the house of his fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And these &#91;are&#93; the names of the men that shall stand with you: of &#91;the tribe of&#93; Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;; Elitzur &#91;My God is a Rock&#93; the son of Sh’de’ur &#91;Almighty’s Light&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> Of Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;; Shlumi’el &#91;Friend of God&#93; the son of Tzuri-shaddai &#91;My Rock is the Almighty&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> Of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; Nachshon &#91;Enchanter&#93; the son of ‘Amminadav &#91;family of the prince&#93;.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Take the sum of the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93; from among the sons of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;, after their families, by the house of their fathers,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> From thirty years old and upward even until fifty years old, all that enter into the host, to do the work in the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> This &#91;shall be&#93; the service of the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93; in the tabernacle of the congregation, &#91;about&#93; the most holy things:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And when the camp sets forward, Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall come, and his sons, and they shall take down the covering veil, and cover the ark of testimony with it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And shall put thereon the covering of badgers’ skins, and shall spread over &#91;it&#93; a cloth wholly of blue, and shall put in the rods (clubs) thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And upon the table of showbread, they shall spread a cloth of blue, and put thereon the dishes, and the spoons, and the bowls, and covers to cover withal: and the continual bread shall be thereon:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And they shall spread upon them a cloth of scarlet, and cover the same with a covering of badgers’ skins, and shall put in the rods (clubs) thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And they shall take a cloth of blue, and cover the candlestick of the light, and his lamps, and his tongs, and his snuffdishes, and all the oil vessels thereof, which they minister unto it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And they shall put it and all the vessels thereof within a covering of badgers’ skins, and shall put &#91;it&#93; upon a bar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And upon the golden altar, they shall spread a cloth of blue, and cover it with a covering of badgers’ skins, and shall put to the rods (clubs) thereof:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And they shall take all the instruments of ministry, which they minister in the sanctuary, and put &#91;them&#93; in a cloth of blue, and cover them with a covering of badgers’ skins, and shall put &#91;them&#93; on a bar:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And they shall take away the ashes from the altar, and spread a purple cloth thereon:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And they shall put upon it all the vessels thereof, which they minister about it, &#91;even&#93; the censers, the flesh hooks, and the shovels, and the basins, all the vessels of the altar; and they shall spread upon it a covering of badgers’ skins, and put to the rods (clubs) of it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And when Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons have made an end of covering the sanctuary, and all the vessels of the sanctuary, as the camp is to set forward; after that, the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93; shall come to bear &#91;it&#93;: but they shall not touch &#91;any&#93; holy thing, lest they die. These &#91;things are&#93; the burden of the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93; in the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And to the office of El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the son of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest &#91;pertains&#93; the oil for the light, and the sweet incense, and the daily meat offering, and the anointing oil, &#91;and&#93; the oversight of all the tabernacle, and of all that in it &#91;is&#93;, in the sanctuary, and in the vessels thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> Cut you2f not off the tribe of the families of the K’hati from among the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> But thus do unto them, that they may live, and not die, when they approach unto the most holy things: Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall go in, and appoint them every one to his service and to his burden:</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> But they shall not go in to see when the holy things are covered, lest they die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> Take also the sum of the sons of Gershon &#91;Exile&#93;, throughout the houses of their fathers, by their families;</span>

<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> From thirty years old and upward until fifty years old shall youi number them; all that enter in to perform the service, to do the work in the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>

<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And they shall bear the curtains of the tabernacle, and the tabernacle of the congregation, his covering, and the covering of the badgers’ skins that &#91;is&#93; above upon it, and the hanging for the door of the tabernacle of the congregation,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And the hangings of the court, and the hanging for the door of the gate of the court, which &#91;is&#93; by the tabernacle and by the altar round about, and their cords, and all the instruments of their service, and all that is made for them: so shall they serve.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> At the appointment of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall be all the service of the sons of the Gershuni &#91;Exiled Ones&#93;, in all their burdens, and in all their service: and you2f shall appoint unto them in charge all their burdens.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> This &#91;is&#93; the service of the families of the sons of Gershon &#91;Exile&#93; in the tabernacle of the congregation: and their charge &#91;shall be&#93; under the hand of Itamar &#91;Coast of Palms&#93; the son of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> As for the sons of M’rari &#91;Bitter&#93;, youi shall number them after their families, by the house of their fathers;</span>

<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> From thirty years old and upward even unto fifty years old shall youi number them, every one that enters into the service, to do the work of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 30:12–13; Genesis 46:27; Genesis 49:20"> Even those that were numbered of them, throughout their families, by the house of their fathers, were two thousand and six hundred and thirty.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44" data-xref="Numbers 26:64; Numbers 1:2–16"> Even those that were numbered of them after their families, were three thousand and two hundred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> These &#91;be&#93; those that were numbered of the families of the sons of M’rari &#91;Bitter&#93;, whom Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; numbered according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="Exodus 12:37; Numbers 26:51; Exodus 38:26"> All those that were numbered of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, whom Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and the chief of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; numbered, after their families, and after the house of their fathers,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Numbers 2:33; Numbers 3:1–49; Numbers 26:57–62"> From thirty years old and upward even unto fifty years old, every one that came to do the service of the ministry, and the service of the burden in the tabernacle of the congregation,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> Even those that were numbered of them, were eight thousand and five hundred and eighty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Numbers 26:62; Numbers 2:33"> According to the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they were numbered by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, every one according to his service, and according to his burden: thus were they numbered of him, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Command the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they put out of the camp every leper, and every one that has an issue (what comes forth), and whosoever is defiled by the dead:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> Both male and female shall you2f put out, without the camp shall you2f put them; that they defile not their camps, in the midst whereof I dwell.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; did so, and put them out without the camp: as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so did the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, When a man or woman shall commit any sin that men commit, to do a trespass against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and that person be guilty;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> Then they shall confess their sin which they have done: and he shall recompense his trespass with the principal thereof, and add unto it the fifth &#91;part&#93; thereof, and give &#91;it&#93; unto &#91;him&#93; against whom he has trespassed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> But if the man have no kinsman to recompense the trespass unto, let the trespass be recompensed unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;even&#93; to the priest; beside the ram of the atonement, whereby an atonement shall be made for him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And every offering of all the holy things of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which they bring unto the priest, shall be his.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And every man’s consecrated things shall be his: whatsoever any man gives the priest, it shall be his.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, If any man’s wife go aside, and commit a trespass against him,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And a man lie with her carnally, and it be hid from the eyes of her husband, and be kept close, and she be defiled, and &#91;there be&#93; no witness against her, neither she be taken &#91;with the manner&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And the spirit of jealousy come upon him, and he be jealous of his wife, and she be defiled: or if the spirit of jealousy come upon him, and he be jealous of his wife, and she be not defiled:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> Then shall the man bring his wife unto the priest, and he shall bring her offering for her, the tenth &#91;part&#93; of an eifah of barley meal; he shall pour no oil upon it, nor put frankincense thereon; for it &#91;is&#93; an offering of jealousy, an offering of memorial, bringing iniquity to remembrance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And the priest shall bring her near, and set her before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And the priest shall take holy water in an earthen vessel; and of the dust that is in the floor of the tabernacle the priest shall take, and put &#91;it&#93; into the water:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And the priest shall set the woman before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and uncover the woman’s head, and put the offering of memorial in her hands, which &#91;is&#93; the jealousy offering: and the priest shall have in his hand the bitter water that causes the curse:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And the priest shall charge her by an oath, and say unto the woman, If no man have lain with youi, and if youi have not gone aside to uncleanness &#91;with another&#93; instead of youi husband, be youi free from this bitter water that causes the curse:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> But if youi have gone aside &#91;to another&#93; instead of youi husband, and if youi be defiled, and some man have lain with youi beside youi husband:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> Then the priest shall charge the woman with an oath of cursing, and the priest shall say unto the woman, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) make youi a curse and an oath among youi people, when the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) does make youi thigh to rot, and youi belly to swell;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And this water that causes the curse shall go into youi bowels (inward parts; affections), to make &#91;youi&#93; belly to swell, and &#91;youi&#93; thigh to rot: And the woman shall say, Amen, amen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> And the priest shall write these curses in a book, and he shall blot &#91;them&#93; out with the bitter water:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> And he shall cause the woman to drink the bitter water that causes the curse: and the water that causes the curse shall enter into her, &#91;and become&#93; bitter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> Then the priest shall take the jealousy offering out of the woman’s hand, and shall wave the offering before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and offer it upon the altar:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And the priest shall take an handful of the offering, &#91;even&#93; the memorial thereof, and burn &#91;it&#93; upon the altar, and afterward shall cause the woman to drink the water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And when he has made her to drink the water, then it shall come to pass, &#91;that&#93;, if she be defiled, and have done trespass against her husband, that the water that causes the curse shall enter into her, &#91;and become&#93; bitter, and her belly shall swell, and her thigh shall rot: and the woman shall be a curse among her people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And if the woman be not defiled, but be clean; then she shall be free, and shall conceive seed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> This &#91;is&#93; the law of jealousies, when a wife goes aside &#91;to another&#93; instead of her husband, and is defiled;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> Or when the spirit of jealousy comes upon him, and he be jealous over his wife, and shall set the woman before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the priest shall execute upon her all this law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> Then shall the man be guiltless from iniquity, and this woman shall bear her iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, When either man or woman shall separate &#91;themselves&#93; to vow a vow of a Nazir &#91;One Separated&#93;, to separate &#91;themselves&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> He shall separate &#91;himself&#93; from wine and strong drink, and shall drink no vinegar of wine, or vinegar of strong drink, neither shall he drink any liquor of grapes, nor eat moist grapes, or dried.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> All the days of his separation shall he eat nothing that is made of the vine tree, from the kernels even to the husk.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> All the days of the vow of his separation there shall no razor come upon his head: until the days be fulfilled, in the which he separates &#91;himself&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), he shall be holy, &#91;and&#93; shall let the locks of the hair of his head grow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> All the days that he separates &#91;himself&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) he shall come at no dead body.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> He shall not make himself unclean for his father, or for his mother, for his brother, or for his sister, when they die: because the consecration of his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; upon his head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> All the days of his separation he &#91;is&#93; holy unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And if any man die very suddenly by him, and he has defiled the head of his consecration; then he shall shave his head in the day of his cleansing, on the seventh day shall he shave it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And on the eighth day he shall bring two turtles, or two young pigeons, to the priest, to the door of the tabernacle of the congregation:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And the priest shall offer the one for a sin offering, and the other for a burnt offering, and make an atonement for him, for that he sinned by the dead, and shall hallow (to approve, commend, accept) his head that same day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And he shall consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) the days of his separation, and shall bring a lamb of the first year for a trespass offering: but the days that were before shall be lost, because his separation was defiled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And this &#91;is&#93; the law of the Nazir &#91;One Separated&#93;, when the days of his separation are fulfilled: he shall be brought unto the door of the tabernacle of the congregation:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And he shall offer his offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), one he lamb of the first year without blemish for a burnt offering, and one ewe lamb of the first year without blemish for a sin offering, and one ram without blemish for peace offerings,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And a basket of unleavened bread, cakes of fine flour mingled with oil, and wafers of unleavened bread anointed with oil, and their meat offering, and their drink offerings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And the priest shall bring &#91;them&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and shall offer his sin offering, and his burnt offering:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And he shall offer the ram &#91;for&#93; a sacrifice of peace offerings unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), with the basket of unleavened bread: the priest shall offer also his meat offering, and his drink offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And the Nazir &#91;One Separated&#93; shall shave the head of his separation &#91;at&#93; the door of the tabernacle of the congregation, and shall take the hair of the head of his separation, and put &#91;it&#93; in the fire which &#91;is&#93; under the sacrifice of the peace offerings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And the priest shall take the coocked (boiled) shoulder of the ram, and one unleavened cake out of the basket, and one unleavened wafer, and shall put &#91;them&#93; upon the hands of the Nazir &#91;One Separated&#93;, after &#91;the hair of&#93; his separation is shaven:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And the priest shall wave them &#91;for&#93; a wave offering before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): this &#91;is&#93; holy for the priest, with the wave breast and heave shoulder: and after that the Nazir &#91;One Separated&#93; may drink wine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> This &#91;is&#93; the law of the Nazir &#91;One Separated&#93; who has vowed, &#91;and of&#93; his offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) for his separation, beside &#91;that&#93; that his hand shall get: according to the vow which he vowed, so he must do after the law of his separation.</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And it came to pass on the day that Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; had fully set up the tabernacle, and had anointed it, and sanctified it, and all the instruments thereof, both the altar and all the vessels thereof, and had anointed them, and sanctified them;</span>

<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> On the seventh day Elishama &#91;My God has Heard&#93; the son of Ammihud &#91;People of Magesty&#93;, prince of the children of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, &#91;offered&#93;:</span>
</div>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54" data-xref="Deuteronomy 12:32; Exodus 40:16; Exodus 39:32"> On the eighth day &#91;offered&#93; Gamali’el &#91;Reward of God&#93; the son of Pedahzur &#91;The Rock has Ransomed&#93;, prince of the children of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;:</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v84" data-v="84"> This &#91;was&#93; the dedication of the altar, in the day when it was anointed, by the princes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: twelve chargers of silver, twelve silver bowls, twelve spoons of gold:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v85" data-v="85"> Each charger (a large platter or dish) of silver &#91;weighing&#93; an hundred and thirty &#91;shekels&#93;, each bowl seventy: all the silver vessels &#91;weighed&#93; two thousand and four hundred &#91;shekels&#93;, after the shekel of the sanctuary:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v86" data-v="86"> The golden spoons &#91;were&#93; twelve, full of incense, &#91;weighing&#93; ten &#91;shekels&#93; apiece, after the shekel of the sanctuary: all the gold of the spoons &#91;was&#93; an hundred and twenty &#91;shekels&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v87" data-v="87"> All the oxen for the burnt offering &#91;were&#93; twelve bullocks, the rams twelve, the lambs of the first year twelve, with their meat offering: and the kids of the goats for sin offering twelve.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v88" data-v="88"> And all the oxen for the sacrifice of the peace offerings &#91;were&#93; twenty and four bullocks, the rams sixty, the he goats sixty, the lambs of the first year sixty. This &#91;was&#93; the dedication of the altar, after that it was anointed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v89" data-v="89"> And when Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; was gone into the tabernacle of the congregation to speak with him, then he heard the voice of one speaking unto him from off the mercy seat that &#91;was&#93; upon the ark of testimony, from between the two cherubim: and he spoke unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Speak unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and say unto him, When youi lightest the lamps, the seven lamps shall give light over against the candlestick.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; did so; he lighted the lamps thereof over against the candlestick, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And this work of the candlestick &#91;was of&#93; beaten gold, unto the shaft thereof, unto the flowers thereof, &#91;was&#93; beaten work: according unto the pattern which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had showed Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so he made the candlestick.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> Take the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; from among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and cleanse them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And thus shall youi do unto them, to cleanse them: Sprinkle water of purifying upon them, and let them shave all their flesh, and let them wash their clothes, and &#91;so&#93; make themselves clean.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> Then let them take a young bullock with his meat offering, &#91;even&#93; fine flour mingled with oil, and another young bullock shall youi take for a sin offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And youi shall bring the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; before the tabernacle of the congregation: and youi shall gather the whole assembly of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; together:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And youi shall bring the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall put their hands upon the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall offer the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;for&#93; an offering of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they may execute the service of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; shall lay their hands upon the heads of the bullocks: and youi shall offer the one &#91;for&#93; a sin offering, and the other &#91;for&#93; a burnt offering, unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to make an atonement for the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And youi shall set the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; before Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and before his sons, and offer them &#91;for&#93; an offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> Thus shall youi separate the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; from among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; shall be my.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And after that shall the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; go in to do the service of the tabernacle of the congregation: and youi shall cleanse them, and offer them &#91;for&#93; an offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> For they &#91;are&#93; wholly given unto me from among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; instead of such as open every womb, &#91;even instead of&#93; the firstborn of all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, have I taken them unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> For all the firstborn of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;are&#93; my, &#91;both&#93; man and beast: on the day that I struck every firstborn in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; I sanctified them for myself.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And I have taken the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; for all the firstborn of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And I have given the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;as&#93; a gift to Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and to his sons from among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to do the service of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in the tabernacle of the congregation, and to make an atonement for the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: that there be no plague among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; come near unto the sanctuary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, did to the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; according unto all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; concerning the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, so did the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; were purified, and they washed their clothes; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; offered them &#91;as&#93; an offering before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; made an atonement for them to cleanse them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And after that went the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; in to do their service in the tabernacle of the congregation before Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and before his sons: as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; concerning the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, so did they unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> This &#91;is it&#93; that &#91;belongs&#93; unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;: from twenty and five years old and upward they shall go in to wait upon the service of the tabernacle of the congregation:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And from the age of fifty years they shall cease waiting upon the service &#91;thereof&#93;, and shall serve no more:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> But shall minister with their brethren in the tabernacle of the congregation, to keep the charge, and shall do no service. Thus shall youi do unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; touching their charge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; in the wilderness of Sinai, in the first month of the second year after they were come out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Let the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; also keep the Passover at his appointed season.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> In the fourteenth day of this month, at even, you2f shall keep it in his appointed season: according to all the rites of it, and according to all the ceremonies thereof, shall you2f keep it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; spoke unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they should keep the Passover.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And they kept the Passover on the fourteenth day of the first month at even in the wilderness of Sinai: according to all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so did the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And there were certain men, who were defiled by the dead body of a man, that they could not keep the Passover on that day: and they came before Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and before Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; on that day:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And those men said unto him, We &#91;are&#93; defiled by the dead body of a man: wherefore are we kept back, that we may not offer an offering of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in his appointed season among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto them, Stand still, and I will hear what the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will command concerning you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, If any man of you or of your posterity shall be unclean by reason of a dead body, or &#91;be&#93; in a journey afar off, yet he shall keep the Passover unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> The fourteenth day of the second month at even they shall keep it, &#91;and&#93; eat it with unleavened bread and bitter &#91;herbs&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> They shall leave none of it unto the morning, nor break any bone of it: according to all the ordinances of the Passover they shall keep it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> But the man that &#91;is&#93; clean, and is not in a journey, and forbears to keep the Passover, even the same soul shall be cut off from among his people: because he brought not the offering of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in his appointed season, that man shall bear his sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And if a stranger shall sojourn among you, and will keep the Passover unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); according to the ordinance of the Passover, and according to the manner thereof, so shall he do: you2f shall have one ordinance, both for the stranger, and for him that was born in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And on the day that the tabernacle was reared up the cloud covered the tabernacle, &#91;namely&#93;, the tent of the testimony: and at even there was upon the tabernacle as it were the appearance of fire, until the morning.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> So it was always: the cloud covered it &#91;by day&#93;, and the appearance of fire by night.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And when the cloud was taken up from the tabernacle, then after that the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; journeyed: and in the place where the cloud abode, there the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; pitched their tents.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> At the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; journeyed, and at the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they pitched: as long as the cloud abode upon the tabernacle they rested in their tents.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And when the cloud tarried long upon the tabernacle many days, then the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; kept the charge of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and journeyed not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And &#91;so&#93; it was, when the cloud was a few days upon the tabernacle; according to the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they abode in their tents, and according to the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they journeyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And &#91;so&#93; it was, when the cloud abode from even unto the morning, and &#91;that&#93; the cloud was taken up in the morning, then they journeyed: whether &#91;it was&#93; by day or by night that the cloud was taken up, they journeyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> Or &#91;whether it were&#93; two days, or a month, or a year, that the cloud tarried upon the tabernacle, remaining thereon, the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; abode in their tents, and journeyed not: but when it was taken up, they journeyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> At the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they rested in the tents, and at the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) they journeyed: they kept the charge of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), at the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Make youi two trumpets of silver; of a whole piece shall youi make them: that youi may use them for the calling of the assembly, and for the journeying of the camps.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And when they shall blow with them, all the assembly shall assemble themselves to youi at the door of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And if they blow &#91;but&#93; with one &#91;trumpet&#93;, then the princes, &#91;which are&#93; heads of the thousands of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, shall gather themselves unto youi.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> When you2f blow an alarm, then the camps that lie on the east parts shall go forward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> When you2f blow an alarm the second time, then the camps that lie on the south side shall take their journey: they shall blow an alarm for their journeys.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> But when the congregation is to be gathered together, you2f shall blow, but you2f shall not sound an alarm.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, the priests, shall blow with the trumpets; and they shall be to you for an ordinance forever throughout your generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And if you2f go to war in your land against the enemy that oppresses you, then you2f shall blow an alarm with the trumpets; and you2f shall be remembered before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and you2f shall be saved from your enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> Also in the day of your gladness, and in your solemn days, and in the beginnings of your months, you2f shall blow with the trumpets over your burnt offerings, and over the sacrifices of your peace offerings; that they may be to you for a memorial before your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And it came to pass on the twentieth &#91;day&#93; of the second month, in the second year, that the cloud was taken up from off the tabernacle of the testimony.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; took their journeys out of the wilderness of Sinai; and the cloud rested in the wilderness of Pa’ran &#91;place of caverns&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And they first took their journey according to the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> In the first &#91;place&#93; went the Flag (banner) of the camp of the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; according to their armies: and over his host &#91;was&#93; Nachshon &#91;Enchanter&#93; the son of ‘Amminadav &#91;family of the prince&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Hovav &#91;Cherished&#93;, the son of Re’u’el &#91;Friend of God&#93; the Midyani &#91;Strife&#93;, Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;’ father in law, We are journeying unto the place of which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said, I will give it you: come youi with us, and we will do youi good: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken good concerning Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> And he said unto him, I will not go; but I will depart to my own land, and to my family.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> And he said, Leave us not, I pray youi; forasmuch as youi know how we are to encamp in the wilderness, and youi may be to us instead of eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> And it shall be, if youi go with us, yes, it shall be, that what goodness the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall do unto us, the same will we do unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 26:37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Numbers 2:19"> And they departed from the mount of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) three days’ journey: and the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) went before them in the three days’ journey, to search out a resting place for them.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Numbers 26:34; Genesis 48:19–20; Numbers 2:21"> And it came to pass, when the ark set forward, that Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, Rise up, LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and let youi enemies be scattered; and let them that hate youi flee before youi.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Genesis 49:27; Genesis 44:20; Revelation 7:8"> And when it rested, he said, Return, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), unto the many thousands of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And &#91;when&#93; the people complained, it displeased the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) heard &#91;it&#93;; and his anger was kindled; and the fire of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) burnt among them, and consumed &#91;them that were&#93; in the uttermost parts of the camp.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And the people cried unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;; and when Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; prayed unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the fire was quenched.</span>
</div>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And the mixt multitude that &#91;was&#93; among them fell a lusting: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; also wept again, and said, Who shall give us flesh to eat?</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> But now our soul &#91;is&#93; dried away: &#91;there is&#93; nothing at all, beside this manna, &#91;before&#93; our eyes.</span>

<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And the manna &#91;was&#93; as coriander seed, and the colour thereof as the colour of bdellium.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And when the dew fell upon the camp in the night, the manna fell upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> Then Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; heard the people weep throughout their families, every man in the door of his tent: and the anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was kindled greatly; Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; also was displeased.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), For what reason have youi afflicted youi servant? and wherefore have I not found favour in youi sight, that youi lays the burden of all this people upon me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> Have I conceived all this people? have I begotten them, that youi should say unto me, Carry them in youi bosom, as a nursing father bears the sucking child, unto the land which youi swore unto their fathers?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> Whence should I have flesh to give unto all this people? for they weep unto me, saying, Give us flesh, that we may eat.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> I am not able to bear all this people alone, because &#91;it is&#93; too heavy for me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And if youi deal thus with me, kill me, I pray youi, out of hand, if I have found favour in youi sight; and let me not see my wretchedness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Gather unto me seventy men of the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, whom youi know to be the elders of the people, and officers over them; and bring them unto the tabernacle of the congregation, that they may stand there with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And I will come down and talk with youi there: and I will take of the spirit which &#91;is&#93; upon youi, and will put &#91;it&#93; upon them; and they shall bear the burden of the people with youi, that youi bear &#91;it&#93; not yourself alone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And say youi unto the people, Sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) yourselves against tomorrow, and you2f shall eat flesh: for you2f have wept in the ears of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying, Who shall give us flesh to eat? for &#91;it was&#93; well with us in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: therefore the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will give you flesh, and you2f shall eat.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> You2f shall not eat one day, nor two days, nor five days, neither ten days, nor twenty days;</span>

<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And wherefore has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought us unto this land, to fall by the sword, that our wives and our children should be a prey? were it not better for us to return into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> Then Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; fell on their faces before all the assembly of the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; the son of Nun &#91;Posterity&#93;, and Kalev &#91;Dog&#93; the son of Y’funeh &#91;He Will Face&#93;, &#91;which were&#93; of them that searched the land, rent their clothes:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And they spoke unto all the company of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, The land, which we passed through to search it, &#91;is&#93; an exceeding good land.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> If the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) delight in us, then he will bring us into this land, and give it us; a land which flows with milk and honey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> Only rebel not you2f against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), neither fear you2f the people of the land; for they &#91;are&#93; bread for us: their defense is departed from them, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; with us: fear them not.</span>
</div>

And the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) appeared in the tabernacle of the congregation before all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, How long will this people provoke me? and how long will it be before they believe me, for all the signs which I have showed among them?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Then the Mitzri shall hear &#91;it&#93;, (for youi brought up this people in youi might from among them;)</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And they will tell &#91;it&#93; to the inhabitants of this land: &#91;for&#93; they have heard that youi LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;are&#93; among this people, that youi LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) are seen face to face, and &#91;that&#93; youri cloud stands over them, and &#91;that&#93; youi go before them, by day time in a pillar of a cloud, and in a pillar of fire by night.</span>

<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> Now &#91;if&#93; youi shall kill &#91;all&#93; this people as one man, then the nations which have heard the fame of youi will speak, saying,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> Because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was not able to bring this people into the land which he swore unto them, therefore he has slain them in the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And now, I beseech (to call upon; appeal; beg) youi, let the power of my Lord be great, according as youi have spoken, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; longsuffering, and of great mercy, forgiving iniquity and transgression, and by no means clearing &#91;the guilty&#93;, visiting the iniquity of the fathers upon the children unto the third and fourth &#91;generation&#93;.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said, I have pardoned according to youi word:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> Because all those men which have seen my glory, and my miracles, which I did in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; and in the wilderness, and have tempted me now these ten times, and have not listened to my voice;</span>

<div className="bp">

I have heard the murmurings of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which they murmur against me.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> Say unto them, &#91;As truly as&#93; I live, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), as you2f have spoken in my ears, so will I do to you:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> Your carcasses shall fall in this wilderness; and all that were numbered of you, according to your whole number, from twenty years old and upward, which have murmured against me,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> Doubtless you2f shall not come into the land, &#91;concerning&#93; which I swore to make you dwell in it, save Kalev &#91;Dog&#93; the son of Y’funeh &#91;He Will Face&#93;, and Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; the son of Nun &#91;Posterity&#93;.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> But your little ones, which you2f said should be a prey, them will I bring in, and they shall know the land which you2f have despised.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 26:28–34"> After the number of the days in which you2f searched the land, &#91;even&#93; forty days, each day for a year, shall you2f bear your iniquities, &#91;even&#93; forty years, and you2f shall know my breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet) of promise.</span>

<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 30:12–13; Genesis 46:27; Genesis 49:20"> And they rose up early in the morning, and got them up into the top of the mountain, saying, Lo, we &#91;be here&#93;, and will go up unto the place which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has promised: for we have sinned.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And will make an offering by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), a burnt offering, or a sacrifice in performing a vow, or in a freewill offering, or in your solemn feasts, to make a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), of the herd, or of the flock:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> Then shall he that offers his offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) bring a meat offering of a tenth deal of flour mingled with the fourth &#91;part&#93; of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of oil.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And the fourth &#91;part&#93; of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of wine for a drink offering shall youi prepare with the burnt offering or sacrifice, for one lamb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> Or for a ram, youi shall prepare &#91;for&#93; a meat offering two tenth deals of flour mingled with the third &#91;part&#93; of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And for a drink offering youi shall offer the third &#91;part&#93; of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of wine, &#91;for&#93; a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And when youi prepare a bullock &#91;for&#93; a burnt offering, or &#91;for&#93; a sacrifice in performing a vow, or peace offerings unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> Then shall he bring with a bullock a meat offering of three tenth deals of flour mingled with half an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And youi shall bring for a drink offering half an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of wine, &#91;for&#93; an offering made by fire, of a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> Thus shall it be done for one bullock, or for one ram, or for a lamb, or a kid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> According to the number that you2f shall prepare, so shall you2f do to every one according to their number.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> All that are born of the country shall do these things after this manner, in offering an offering made by fire, of a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And if a stranger sojourn with you, or whosoever &#91;be&#93; among you in your generations, and will offer an offering made by fire, of a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); as you2f do, so he shall do.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> One ordinance &#91;shall be both&#93; for you of the congregation, and also for the stranger that sojournes &#91;with you&#93;, an ordinance forever in your generations: as you2f &#91;are&#93;, so shall the stranger be before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> Then it shall be, that, when you2f eat of the bread of the land, you2f shall offer up an heave offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> Of the first of your dough you2f shall give unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) an heave offering in your generations.</span>
