<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And if you2f have erred, and not observed all these commandments, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has spoken unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> &#91;Even&#93; all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has commanded you by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, from the day that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded &#91;Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;&#93;, and henceforward among your generations;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> Then it shall be, if &#91;ought&#93; be committed by ignorance without the knowledge of the congregation, that all the congregation shall offer one young bullock for a burnt offering, for a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), with his meat offering, and his drink offering, according to the manner, and one kid of the goats for a sin offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And the priest shall make an atonement for all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and it shall be forgiven them; for it &#91;is&#93; ignorance: and they shall bring their offering, a sacrifice made by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and their sin offering before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), for their ignorance:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And it shall be forgiven all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and the stranger that sojournes among them; seeing all the people &#91;were&#93; in ignorance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And if any soul sin through ignorance, then he shall bring a she goat of the first year for a sin offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And the priest shall make an atonement for the soul that sins ignorantly, when he sins by ignorance before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to make an atonement for him; and it shall be forgiven him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> You2f shall have one law for him that sins through ignorance, &#91;both for&#93; him that is born among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and for the stranger that sojournes among them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> But the soul that does &#91;ought&#93; presumptuously, &#91;whether he be&#93; born in the land, or a stranger, the same reproaches the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and that soul shall be cut off from among his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> Because he has despised the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and has broken his commandment, that soul shall utterly be cut off; his iniquity &#91;shall be&#93; upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> And while the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; were in the wilderness, they found a man that gathered sticks upon the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 26:37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Numbers 2:19"> And they that found him gathering sticks brought him unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and unto all the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 26:28–34"> And they put him in ward (in custody, with a guard; prison), because it was not declared what should be done to him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Numbers 26:34; Genesis 48:19–20; Numbers 2:21"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, The man shall be surely put to death: all the congregation shall stone him with stones without the camp.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Genesis 49:27; Genesis 44:20; Revelation 7:8"> And all the congregation brought him without the camp, and stoned him with stones, and he died; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Numbers 26:41; Judges 20:44–46; 2 Chronicles 17:17"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Genesis 46:23; Genesis 30:5–6; Genesis 49:16–17"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and bid them that they make them fringes in the borders of their garments throughout their generations, and that they put upon the fringe of the borders a ribbon of blue:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Numbers 26:43; Numbers 2:26"> And it shall be unto you for a fringe, that you2f may look upon it, and remember all the commandments of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and do them; and that you2f seek not after your own heart and your own eyes, after which you2f use to go a whoring:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 30:12–13; Genesis 46:27; Genesis 49:20"> That you2f may remember, and do all my commandments, and be holy unto your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Numbers 26:47; Numbers 2:28"> I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, which brought you out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, to be your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> Now Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, the son of Yitz’har &#91;Shining Oil&#93;, the son of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93;, the son of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;, and Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93; and Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93;, the sons of ‘Eli’av &#91;My Father is God&#93;, and On, the son of Pelet &#91;Swiftness&#93;, sons of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, took &#91;men&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And they rose up before Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, with certain of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, two hundred and fifty princes of the assembly, famous in the congregation, men of renown (great reputation; being well-known):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And they gathered themselves together against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and against Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and said unto them, &#91;You2f take&#93; too much upon you, seeing all the congregation &#91;are&#93; holy, every one of them, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; among them: wherefore then lift you2f up yourselves above the congregation of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate)?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And when Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; heard &#91;it&#93;, he fell upon his face:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And he spoke unto Korakh &#91;Bald&#93; and unto all his company, saying, Even tomorrow the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will show who &#91;are&#93; his, and &#91;who is&#93; holy; and will cause &#91;him&#93; to come near unto him: even &#91;him&#93; whom he has chosen will he cause to come near unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> This do; Take you censers, Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, and all his company;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And put fire in it, and put incense in them before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) tomorrow: and it shall be &#91;that&#93; the man whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) does choose, he &#91;shall be&#93; holy: &#91;you2f take&#93; too much upon you, you2f sons of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, Hear, I pray you, you2f sons of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> &#91;Seems it but&#93; a small thing unto you, that the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; has separated you from the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to bring you near to himself to do the service of the tabernacle of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and to stand before the congregation to minister unto them?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And he has brought youi near &#91;to him&#93;, and all youi brethren the sons of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93; with youi: and seek you2f the priesthood also?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> For which cause &#91;both&#93; youi and all youi company &#91;are&#93; gathered together against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and what &#91;is&#93; Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, that you2f murmur against him?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; sent to call Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93; and Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93;, the sons of ‘Eli’av &#91;My Father is God&#93;: which said, We will not come up:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> &#91;Is it&#93; a small thing that youi have brought us up out of a land that flows with milk and honey, to kill us in the wilderness, except youi make yourself altogether a prince over us?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> Moreover youi have not brought us into a land that flows with milk and honey, or given us inheritance of fields and vineyards: will youi put out the eyes of these men? we will not come up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; was very exceedingly angry, and said unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), Respect not youi their offering: I have not taken one ass from them, neither have I hurt one of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, Be youi and all youi company before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), youi, and they, and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, tomorrow:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And take every man his censer, and put incense in them, and bring you2f before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) every man his censer, two hundred and fifty censers; youi also, and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, each &#91;of you&#93; his censer.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And they took every man his censer, and put fire in them, and laid incense thereon, and stood in the door of the tabernacle of the congregation with Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And Korakh &#91;Bald&#93; gathered all the congregation against them unto the door of the tabernacle of the congregation: and the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) appeared unto all the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> Separate yourselves from among this congregation, that I may consume them in a moment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And they fell upon their faces, and said, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of the spirits of all flesh, shall one man sin, and will youi be exceedingly angry with all the congregation?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> Speak unto the congregation, saying, Get you up from about the tabernacle of Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93;, and Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; rose up and went unto Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93; and Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93;; and the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; followed him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And he spoke unto the congregation, saying, Depart, I pray you, from the tents of these wicked men, and touch nothing of theirs, lest you2f be consumed in all their sins.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> So they got up from the tabernacle of Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93;, and Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93;, on every side: and Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93; and Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93; came out, and stood in the door of their tents, and their wives, and their sons, and their little children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, Hereby you2f shall know that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has sent me to do all these works; for &#91;I have&#93; not &#91;done them&#93; of my own mind.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> If these men die the common death of all men, or if they be visited after the visitation of all men; &#91;then&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has not sent me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> But if the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) make a new thing, and the earth open her mouth, and swallow (to approve, commend, accept) them up, with all that &#91;appertain (to belong to; to pertain to)&#93; unto them, and they go down quick into the pit; then you2f shall understand that these men have provoked the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> And it came to pass, as he had made an end of speaking all these words, that the ground clave asunder that &#91;was&#93; under them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> And the earth opened her mouth, and swallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed them up, and their houses, and all the men that &#91;appertained&#93; unto Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, and all &#91;their&#93; goods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 26:37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Numbers 2:19"> They, and all that &#91;appertained&#93; to them, went down alive into the pit, and the earth closed upon them: and they perished from among the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 26:28–34"> And all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that &#91;were&#93; round about them fled at the cry of them: for they said, Lest the earth swallow (to approve, commend, accept) us up &#91;also&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Numbers 26:34; Genesis 48:19–20; Numbers 2:21"> And there came out a fire from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and consumed the two hundred and fifty men that offered incense.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Genesis 49:27; Genesis 44:20; Revelation 7:8"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Numbers 26:41; Judges 20:44–46; 2 Chronicles 17:17"> Speak unto El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the son of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest, that he take up the censers out of the burning, and scatter youi the fire yonder; for they are consecrated.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Genesis 46:23; Genesis 30:5–6; Genesis 49:16–17"> The censers of these sinners against their own souls, let them make them broad plates &#91;for&#93; a covering of the altar: for they offered them before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), therefore they are consecrated: and they shall be a sign unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Numbers 26:43; Numbers 2:26"> And El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the priest took the brazen censers, which they that were burnt had offered; and they were made broad &#91;plates for&#93; a covering of the altar:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 30:12–13; Genesis 46:27; Genesis 49:20"> &#91;To be&#93; a memorial unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that no stranger, which &#91;is&#93; not of the seed of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, come near to offer incense before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); that he be not as Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;, and as his company: as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said to him by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Numbers 26:47; Numbers 2:28"> But on the morrow all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; murmured against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and against Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, saying, You2f have killed the people of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Genesis 49:21; Numbers 26:48–50; Genesis 46:24"> And it came to pass, when the congregation was gathered against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and against Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, that they looked toward the tabernacle of the congregation: and, behold, the cloud covered it, and the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) appeared.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="Numbers 26:50; Numbers 2:30"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; came before the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44" data-xref="Numbers 26:64; Numbers 1:2–16"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> Get you up from among this congregation, that I may consume them as in a moment. And they fell upon their faces.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="Exodus 12:37; Numbers 26:51; Exodus 38:26"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, Take a censer, and put fire in it from off the altar, and put on incense, and go quickly unto the congregation, and make an atonement for them: for there is wrath gone out from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); the plague is begun.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Numbers 2:33; Numbers 3:1–49; Numbers 26:57–62"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; took as Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; commanded, and ran into the midst of the congregation; and, behold, the plague was begun among the people: and he put on incense, and made an atonement for the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And he stood between the dead and the living; and the plague was detained (held).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Numbers 26:62; Numbers 2:33"> Now they that died in the plague were fourteen thousand and seven hundred, beside them that died about the matter of Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="Exodus 38:21; Exodus 31:18; Numbers 4:15"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; returned unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; unto the door of the tabernacle of the congregation: and the plague was detained (held).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and take of every one of them a rod according to the house of &#91;their&#93; fathers, of all their princes according to the house of their fathers twelve rods: write youi every man’s name upon his rod.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And youi shall write Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s name upon the rod of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;: for one rod &#91;shall be&#93; for the head of the house of their fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And youi shall lay them up in the tabernacle of the congregation before the testimony, where I will meet with you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And it shall come to pass, &#91;that&#93; the man’s rod, whom I shall choose, shall blossom: and I will make to cease from me the murmurings of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, whereby they murmur against you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; spoke unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and every one of their princes gave him a rod apiece, for each prince one, according to their fathers’ houses, &#91;even&#93; twelve rods: and the rod of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; &#91;was&#93; among their rods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; laid up the rods before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the tabernacle of witness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And it came to pass, that on the morrow Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; went into the tabernacle of witness; and, behold, the rod of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; for the house of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93; was budded, and brought forth buds, and bloomed blossoms, and yielded almonds.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; brought out all the rods from before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and they looked, and took every man his rod.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Bring Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s rod again before the testimony, to be kept for a token against the rebels; and youi shall quite take away their murmurings from me, that they die not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; did &#91;so&#93;: as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded him, so did he.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying, Behold, we die, we perish, we all perish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> Whosoever comes anything near unto the tabernacle of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall die: shall we be consumed with dying?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, youi and youi sons and youi father’s house with youi shall bear the iniquity of the sanctuary: and youi and youi sons with youi shall bear the iniquity of your priesthood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And youi brethren also of the tribe of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;, the tribe of youi father, bring youi with youi, that they may be joined unto youi, and minister unto youi: but youi and youi sons with youi &#91;shall minister&#93; before the tabernacle of witness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And they shall keep youi charge, and the charge of all the tabernacle: only they shall not come near the vessels of the sanctuary and the altar, that neither they, nor you2f also, die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And they shall be joined unto youi, and keep the charge of the tabernacle of the congregation, for all the service of the tabernacle: and a stranger shall not come near unto you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And you2f shall keep the charge of the sanctuary, and the charge of the altar: that there be no wrath any more upon the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And I, behold, I have taken your brethren the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; from among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: to you &#91;they are&#93; given &#91;as&#93; a gift for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to do the service of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> Therefore youi and youi sons with youi shall keep your priest’s office for everything of the altar, and within the veil; and you2f shall serve: I have given your priest’s office &#91;unto you&#93; as a service of gift: and the stranger that comes near shall be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, Behold, I also have given youi the charge of my heave offerings of all the consecrated things of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; unto youi have I given them by reason of the anointing, and to youi sons, by an ordinance forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> This shall be youi of the most holy things, &#91;reserved&#93; from the fire: every oblation of theirs, every meat offering of theirs, and every sin offering of theirs, and every trespass offering of theirs, which they shall render unto me, &#91;shall be&#93; most holy for youi and for youi sons.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> In the most holy &#91;place&#93; shall youi eat it; every male shall eat it: it shall be holy unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And this &#91;is&#93; youi; the heave offering of their gift, with all the wave offerings of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: I have given them unto youi, and to youi sons and to youi daughters with youi, by a statute forever: every one that is clean in youi house shall eat of it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> All the best of the oil, and all the best of the wine, and of the wheat, the firstfruits of them which they shall offer unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), them have I given youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> &#91;And&#93; whatsoever is first ripe in the land, which they shall bring unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), shall be youi; every one that is clean in youi house shall eat &#91;of&#93; it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> Eveeverything devoted in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall be youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> Eveeverything that opens the womb in all flesh, which they bring unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;whether it be&#93; of men or beasts, shall be youi: nevertheless the firstborn of man shall youi surely redeem, and the firstling of unclean beasts shall youi redeem.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And those that are to be redeemed from a month old shall youi redeem, according to youi estimation, for the money of five shekels, after the shekel of the sanctuary, which &#91;is&#93; twenty gerahs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> But the firstling of a cow, or the firstling of a sheep, or the firstling of a goat, youi shall not redeem; they &#91;are&#93; holy: youi shall sprinkle their blood upon the altar, and shall burn their fat &#91;for&#93; an offering made by fire, for a soothing aroma unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And the flesh of them shall be youi, as the wave breast and as the right shoulder are youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> All the heave offerings of the holy things, which the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; offer unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), have I given youi, and youi sons and youi daughters with youi, by a statute forever: it &#91;is&#93; a covenant of salt forever before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto youi and to youi seed with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, youi shall have no inheritance in their land, neither shall youi have any part among them: I &#91;am&#93; youi part and youi inheritance among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And, behold, I have given the children of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93; all the tenth in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; for an inheritance, for their service which they serve, &#91;even&#93; the service of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> Neither must the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; from this time forth (from now on) come near the tabernacle of the congregation, lest they bear sin, and die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> But the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; shall do the service of the tabernacle of the congregation, and they shall bear their iniquity: &#91;it shall be&#93; a statute forever throughout your generations, that among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; they have no inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> But the tithes of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which they offer &#91;as&#93; an heave offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), I have given to the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; to inherit: therefore I have said unto them, Among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; they shall have no inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> Thus speak unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, and say unto them, When you2f take of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; the tithes which I have given you from them for your inheritance, then you2f shall offer up an heave offering of it for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;even&#93; a tenth &#91;part&#93; of the tithe.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And &#91;this&#93; your heave offering shall be reckoned unto you, as though &#91;it were&#93; the corn of the threshing floor, and as the fullness of the winepress.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> Thus you2f also shall offer an heave offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) of all your tithes, which you2f receive of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and you2f shall give thereof the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; heave offering to Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> Out of all your gifts you2f shall offer every heave offering of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), of all the best thereof, &#91;even&#93; the consecrated part thereof out of it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> Therefore youi shall say unto them, When you2f have heaved the best thereof from it, then it shall be counted unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; as the increase of the threshing floor, and as the increase of the winepress.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> And you2f shall eat it in every place, you2f and your households: for it &#91;is&#93; your reward for your service in the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> And you2f shall bear no sin by reason of it, when you2f have heaved from it the best of it: neither shall you2f pollute the holy things of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, lest you2f die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> This &#91;is&#93; the ordinance of the law which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has commanded, saying, Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they bring youi a red heifer without spot, wherein &#91;is&#93; no blemish, &#91;and&#93; upon which never came yoke:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And you2f shall give her unto El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the priest, that he may bring her forth without the camp, and &#91;one&#93; shall slay her before his face:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the priest shall take of her blood with his finger, and sprinkle of her blood directly before the tabernacle of the congregation seven times:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And &#91;one&#93; shall burn the heifer in his sight; her skin, and her flesh, and her blood, with her dung, shall he burn:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And the priest shall take cedar wood, and hyssop, and scarlet, and cast &#91;it&#93; into the midst of the burning of the heifer.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> Then the priest shall wash his clothes, and he shall bathe his flesh in water, and afterward he shall come into the camp, and the priest shall be unclean until the even.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And he that burns her shall wash his clothes in water, and bathe his flesh in water, and shall be unclean until the even.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And a man &#91;that is&#93; clean shall gather up the ashes of the heifer, and lay &#91;them&#93; up without the camp in a clean place, and it shall be kept for the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; for a water of separation: it &#91;is&#93; a purification for sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And he that gathers the ashes of the heifer shall wash his clothes, and be unclean until the evening: and it shall be unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and unto the stranger that sojournes among them, for a statute forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> He that touches the dead body of any man shall be unclean seven days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> He shall purify himself with it on the third day, and on the seventh day he shall be clean: but if he purify not himself the third day, then the seventh day he shall not be clean.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> Whosoever touches the dead body of any man that is dead, and purifies not himself, defiles the tabernacle of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and that soul shall be cut off from Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: because the water of separation was not sprinkled upon him, he shall be unclean; his uncleanness &#91;is&#93; yet upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> This &#91;is&#93; the law, when a man dies in a tent: all that come into the tent, and all that &#91;is&#93; in the tent, shall be unclean seven days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And every open vessel, which has no covering bound upon it, &#91;is&#93; unclean.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And whosoever touches one that is slain with a sword in the open fields, or a dead body, or a bone of a man, or a grave, shall be unclean seven days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And for an unclean &#91;person&#93; they shall take of the ashes of the burnt heifer of purification for sin, and running water shall be put thereto in a vessel:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And a clean person shall take hyssop, and dip &#91;it&#93; in the water, and sprinkle &#91;it&#93; upon the tent, and upon all the vessels, and upon the persons that were there, and upon him that touched a bone, or one slain, or one dead, or a grave:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And the clean &#91;person&#93; shall sprinkle upon the unclean on the third day, and on the seventh day: and on the seventh day he shall purify himself, and wash his clothes, and bathe himself in water, and shall be clean at even.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> But the man that shall be unclean, and shall not purify himself, that soul shall be cut off from among the congregation, because he has defiled the sanctuary of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): the water of separation has not been sprinkled upon him; he &#91;is&#93; unclean.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And it shall be a perpetual statute unto them, that he that sprinkles the water of separation shall wash his clothes; and he that touches the water of separation shall be unclean until even.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And whatsoever the unclean &#91;person&#93; touches shall be unclean; and the soul that touches &#91;it&#93; shall be unclean until even.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> Then came the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;even&#93; the whole congregation, into the desert of Tzin &#91;Flat&#93; in the first month: and the people abode in Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93;; and Miryam &#91;rebellion&#93; died there, and was buried there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And there was no water for the congregation: and they gathered themselves together against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and against Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And the people chastised with Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and spoke, saying, Would God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; that we had died when our brethren died before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate)!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And why have you2f brought up the congregation of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) into this wilderness, that we and our cattle should die there?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And wherefore have you2f made us to come up out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, to bring us in unto this evil place? it &#91;is&#93; no place of seed, or of figs, or of vines, or of pomegranates; neither &#91;is&#93; there any water to drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; went from the presence of the assembly unto the door of the tabernacle of the congregation, and they fell upon their faces: and the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) appeared unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> Take the rod, and gather youi the assembly together, youi, and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; youi brother, and speak you2f unto the rock before their eyes; and it shall give forth his water, and youi shall bring forth to them water out of the rock: so youi shall give the congregation and their beasts drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; took the rod from before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), as he commanded him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; gathered the congregation together before the rock, and he said unto them, Hear now, you2f rebels; must we fetch you water out of this rock?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; lifted up his hand, and with his rod he struck the rock twice: and the water came out abundantly, and the congregation drank, and their beasts &#91;also&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, Because you2f believed me not, to sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) me in the eyes of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, therefore you2f shall not bring this congregation into the land which I have given them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> This &#91;is&#93; the water of M’rivah &#91;Arguing&#93;; because the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; strove with the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and he was sanctified in them.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And when we cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), he heard our voice, and sent an angel, and has brought us forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: and, behold, we &#91;are&#93; in Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93;, a city in the uttermost of youi border:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> Let us pass, I pray youi, through youi country: we will not pass through the fields, or through the vineyards, neither will we drink &#91;of&#93; the water of the wells: we will go by the king’s &#91;high&#93; way, we will not turn to the right hand nor to the left, until we have passed youi borders.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And Edom &#91;Red&#93; said unto him, youi shall not pass by me, lest I come out against youi with the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; said unto him, We will go by the high way: and if I and my cattle drink of youi water, then I will pay for it: I will only, without &#91;doing&#93; anything &#91;else&#93;, go through on my feet.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And he said, youi shall not go through. And Edom &#91;Red&#93; came out against him with much people, and with a strong hand.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> Thus Edom &#91;Red&#93; refused to give Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; passage through his border: wherefore Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; turned away from him.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> Take Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; his son, and bring them up unto mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93;:</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And strip Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; of his garments, and put them upon El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; his son: and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall be gathered &#91;unto his people&#93;, and shall die there.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; vowed a vow unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and said, If youi will indeed deliver this people into my hand, then I will utterly destroy their cities.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) listened to the voice of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and delivered up the Kena’ani &#91;Merchant&#93;; and they utterly destroyed them and their cities: and he called the name of the place Hormah &#91;Asylum&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And they journeyed from mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93; by the way of the Red sea, to compass (go around, encircle; encompass) the land of Edom &#91;Red&#93;: and the soul of the people was much discouraged because of the way.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And the people spoke against God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, For what reason have you2f brought us up out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; to die in the wilderness? for &#91;there is&#93; no bread, neither &#91;is there any&#93; water; and our soul loathes this light bread.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) sent fiery serpents among the people, and they bit the people; and much people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; died.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; prayed for the people.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Make youi a fiery serpent, and set it upon a pole: and it shall come to pass, that every one that is bitten, when he looks upon it, shall live.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; made a serpent of brass, and put it upon a pole, and it came to pass, that if a serpent had bitten any man, when he beheld the serpent of brass, he lived.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And they journeyed from Ovot &#91;Waterskins&#93;, and pitched at ‘Iyei-Ha’avarim &#91;Ruins of Regions Beyond&#93;, in the wilderness which &#91;is&#93; before Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, toward the sunrising.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> From there they removed, and pitched in the Vadi Zared &#91;“Flexible Twig of a Willow Tree” Brook&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; sent messengers unto Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; king of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> Let me pass through youi land: we will not turn into the fields, or into the vineyards; we will not drink &#91;of&#93; the waters of the well: &#91;but&#93; we will go along by the king’s &#91;high&#93; way, until we be past youi borders.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> And Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; would not suffer Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to pass through his border: but Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; gathered all his people together, and went out against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; into the wilderness: and he came to Yachatz &#91;Trampled Down&#93;, and fought against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> And Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; struck him with the edge of the sword, and possessed his land from ‘Arnon &#91;Rushing Stream&#93; unto Yabok &#91;luxuriant emptying river&#93;, even unto the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;: for the border of the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; &#91;was&#93; strong.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; took all these cities: and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; dwelt in all the cities of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, in Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93;, and in all the villages thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> For Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93; &#91;was&#93; the city of Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; the king of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, who had fought against the former king of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, and taken all his land out of his hand, even unto ‘Arnon &#91;Rushing Stream&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> For what reason they that speak in proverbs say, Come into Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93;, let the city of Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; be built and prepared:</span>

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> For there is a fire gone out of Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93;, a flame from the city of Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93;: it has consumed ‘Ar &#91;A City&#93; of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, &#91;and&#93; the lords of the high places of ‘Arnon &#91;Rushing Stream&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> Woe to youi, Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;! youi are undone, O people of K’mosh &#91;Subduer&#93;: he has given his sons that escaped, and his daughters, into captivity unto Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; king of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> Thus Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; dwelt in the land of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;.</span>

</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> He sent messengers therefore unto Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; the son of B’or &#91;Burning&#93; to P’tor &#91;Soothsayer (one who fortells the future by ungodly means)&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; by the river of the land of the children of his people, to call him, saying, Behold, there is a people come out from Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: behold, they cover the face of the earth, and they abide over against me:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> Come now therefore, I pray youi, curse me this people; for they &#91;are&#93; too mighty for me: perhaps I shall prevail, &#91;that&#93; we may strike them, and &#91;that&#93; I may drive them out of the land: for I know that he whom youi bless &#91;is&#93; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous), and he whom youi curse is cursed.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And the elders of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; and the elders of Midyan &#91;Strife&#93; departed with the rewards of divination in their hand; and they came unto Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93;, and spoke unto him the words of Balak &#91;Devastator&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And he said unto them, Lodge here this night, and I will bring you word again, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall speak unto me: and the princes of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; abode with Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; rose up in the morning, and said unto the princes of Balak &#91;Devastator&#93;, Get you into your land: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) refuses to give me leave to go with you.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; answered and said unto the servants of Balak &#91;Devastator&#93;, If Balak &#91;Devastator&#93; would give me his house full of silver and gold, I cannot go beyond the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to do less or more.</span>

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) opened the mouth of the ass, and she said unto Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93;, What have I done unto youi, that youi have smitten me these three times?</span>

<div className="bp">

And he said, No.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> Then the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) opened the eyes of Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93;, and he saw the angel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) standing in the way, and his sword drawn in his hand: and he bowed down his head, and fell flat on his face.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And he brought him into the field of Tzofim &#91;Watchers&#93;, to the top of Pisgah &#91;Cleft (split hoof; an opening, break or split) Mountain Range&#93;, and built seven altars, and offered a bullock and a ram on &#91;every&#93; altar.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; not a man, that he should lie; neither the Son of man, that he should repent: has he said, and shall he not do &#91;it&#93;? or has he spoken, and shall he not make it good?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> Behold, I have received &#91;commandment&#93; to bless: and he has Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous); and I cannot reverse it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> He has not beheld iniquity in Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, neither has he seen perverseness in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; with him, and the shout of a king &#91;is&#93; among them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; brought them out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; he has as it were the strength of an unicorn.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> Surely &#91;there is&#93; no enchantment against Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, neither &#91;is there&#93; any divination against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: according to this time it shall be said of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; and of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, What has God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; wrought!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> Behold, the people shall rise up as a great lion, and lift up himself as a young lion: he shall not lie down until he eat &#91;of&#93; the prey, and drink the blood of the slain.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And Balak &#91;Devastator&#93; said unto Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93;, Neither curse them at all, nor bless them at all.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> But Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; answered and said unto Balak &#91;Devastator&#93;, Told not I youi, saying, All that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) speaks, that I must do?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And Balak &#91;Devastator&#93; said unto Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93;, Come, I pray youi, I will bring youi unto another place; perhaps it will please God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; that youi may curse me them from there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And Balak &#91;Devastator&#93; brought Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; unto the top of P’or &#91;Cleft (split hoof; an opening, break or split)&#93;, that looks toward Y’shim’on &#91;Wasteland&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> And Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; said unto Balak &#91;Devastator&#93;, Build me here seven altars, and prepare me here seven bullocks and seven rams.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> And Balak &#91;Devastator&#93; did as Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; had said, and offered a bullock and a ram on &#91;every&#93; altar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And when Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; saw that it pleased the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to bless Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, he went not, as at other times, to seek for enchantments, but he set his face toward the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; lifted up his eyes, and he saw Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; abiding &#91;in his tents&#93; according to their tribes; and the spirit of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; came upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And he took up his parable, and said, Bil’am &#91;Not of the People&#93; the son of B’or &#91;Burning&#93; has said, and the man whose eyes are open has said:</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> As the valleys are they spread forth, as gardens by the river’s side, as the trees of lign &#91;brown&#93; aloes which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has planted, &#91;and&#93; as cedar trees beside the waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; he that blesses youi, and cursed &#91;is&#93; he that curses youi.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And when he looked on ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93;, he took up his parable, and said, ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93; &#91;was&#93; the first of the nations; but his latter end &#91;shall be&#93; that he perish forever.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> Our father died in the wilderness, and he was not in the company of them that gathered themselves together against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the company of Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;; but died in his own sin, and had no sons.</span>

</div>

<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Command the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, My offering, &#91;and&#93; my bread for my sacrifices made by fire, &#91;for&#93; a soothing aroma unto me, shall you2f observe to offer unto me in their due season.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And youi shall say unto them, This &#91;is&#93; the offering made by fire which you2f shall offer unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); two lambs of the first year without spot day by day, &#91;for&#93; a continual burnt offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> The one lamb shall youi offer in the morning, and the other lamb shall youi offer at even;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And a tenth &#91;part&#93; of an eifah of flour for a meat offering, mingled with the fourth &#91;part&#93; of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of beaten oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> &#91;It is&#93; a continual burnt offering, which was ordained in mount Sinai for a soothing aroma, a sacrifice made by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> If a man vow a vow unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), or swear an oath to bind his soul with a bond; he shall not break his word, he shall do according to all that proceeds out of his mouth.</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> But if her husband disallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed her on the day that he heard &#91;it&#93;; then he shall make her vow which she vowed, and that which she uttered with her lips, which she bound her soul, of none effect: and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall forgive her.</span>
