</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> For we will not inherit with them on yonder side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, or forward; because our inheritance is fallen to us on this side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; eastward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto them, If you2f will do this thing, if you2f will go armed before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to war,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And will go all of you armed over Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), until he has driven out his enemies from before him,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And the land be subdued before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): then afterward you2f shall return, and be guiltless before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and before Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and this land shall be your possession before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> But if you2f will not do so, behold, you2f have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and be sure your sin will find you out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> Build you cities for your little ones, and folds for your sheep; and do that which has proceeded out of your mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And the children of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; and the children of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93; spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying, You1s servants will do as my lord commands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> Our little ones, our wives, our flocks, and all our cattle, shall be there in the cities of Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> But youi servants will pass over, every man armed for war, before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to battle, as my lord says.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> So concerning them Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; commanded El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the priest, and Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; the son of Nun &#91;Posterity&#93;, and the chief fathers of the tribes of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto them, If the children of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; and the children of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93; will pass with you over Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, every man armed to battle, before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and the land shall be subdued before you; then you2f shall give them the land of Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93; for a possession:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> But if they will not pass over with you armed, they shall have possessions among you in the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> And the children of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; and the children of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93; answered, saying, As the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has said unto youi servants, so will we do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> We will pass over armed before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) into the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;, that the possession of our inheritance on this side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; &#91;may be&#93; ours.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 26:37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Numbers 2:19"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; gave unto them, &#91;even&#93; to the children of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, and to the children of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, and unto half the tribe of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; the son of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, the kingdom of Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; king of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, and the kingdom of ‘Og &#91;Long-necked&#93; king of Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;, the land, with the cities thereof in the coasts, &#91;even&#93; the cities of the country round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 26:28–34"> And the children of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; built Divon &#91;Wasting&#93;, and ‘Atarot &#91;Crowns&#93;, and ‘Aeo’er &#91;Ruins&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Numbers 26:34; Genesis 48:19–20; Numbers 2:21"> And ‘Artrot-Shofan &#91;Crowns in Their Rapine&#93;, and Ya’zer &#91;Helped&#93;, and Yogbehah &#91;Lofty&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Genesis 49:27; Genesis 44:20; Revelation 7:8"> And Beit-Nimrah &#91;House of the Leopard&#93;, and Beit-Haran &#91;mountaineer&#93; &#91;House of the Joyful Shouter&#93;, fenced cities: and folds for sheep.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Numbers 26:41; Judges 20:44–46; 2 Chronicles 17:17"> And the children of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93; built Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93;, and El’aleh &#91;God is Ascending&#93;, and Kiryatayim &#91;Two Cities&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Genesis 46:23; Genesis 30:5–6; Genesis 49:16–17"> And Nevo &#91;Prophet&#93;, and Ba’al-M’on &#91;lord of the Habitation&#93;, (their names being changed,) and Sivmah &#91;Fragrance&#93;: and gave other names unto the cities which they built.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Numbers 26:43; Numbers 2:26"> And the children of Makhir &#91;Sold&#93; the son of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; went to Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;, and took it, and dispossessed the Emori &#91;Rebellious Highlanders&#93; which &#91;was&#93; in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 30:12–13; Genesis 46:27; Genesis 49:20"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; gave Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93; unto Makhir &#91;Sold&#93; the son of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;; and he dwelt in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Numbers 26:47; Numbers 2:28"> And Ya’ir &#91;He Enlightens&#93; the son of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; went and took the small to (to do entirely, do wholly)wns thereof, and called them Havot-Ya’ir &#91;Villages of the Enlighteners&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Genesis 49:21; Numbers 26:48–50; Genesis 46:24"> And Novach &#91;Barking&#93; went and took K’nat &#91;Possession&#93;, and the villages thereof, and called it Novach &#91;Barking&#93;, after his own name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> These &#91;are&#93; the journeys of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which went forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; with their armies under the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; wrote their goings out according to their journeys by the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and these &#91;are&#93; their journeys according to their goings out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And they departed from Ra’amses in the first month, on the fifteenth day of the first month; on the morrow after the Passover the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; went out with an high hand in the sight of all the Mitzri.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> For the Mitzri buried all &#91;their&#93; firstborn, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had smitten among them: upon their gods also the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) executed judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; removed from Ra’amses, and pitched in Sukkot &#91;Booths&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And they departed from Sukkot &#91;Booths&#93;, and pitched in ‘Eitam &#91;Lair of Wild Beasts&#93; &#91;with them: their plowshare&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; in the edge of the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And they removed from ‘Eitam &#91;Lair of Wild Beasts&#93; &#91;with them: their plowshare&#93;, and turned again unto Pi-Hachirot &#91;The Place where the Sedge Grows&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; before Ba’al-Tz’fon &#91;Lord of the North&#93;: and they pitched before Migdol &#91;Tower&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And they departed from before Pi-Hachirot &#91;The Place where the Sedge Grows&#93;, and passed through the midst of the sea into the wilderness, and went three days’ journey in the wilderness of ‘Eitam &#91;Lair of Wild Beasts&#93; &#91;with them: their plowshare&#93;, and pitched in Marah &#91;Bitterness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And they removed from Marah &#91;Bitterness&#93;, and came unto Eilim &#91;Palms&#93;: and in Eilim &#91;Palms&#93; &#91;were&#93; twelve fountains of water, and threescore and ten palm trees; and they pitched there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And they removed from Eilim &#91;Palms&#93;, and encamped by the Red sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And they removed from the Red sea, and encamped in the wilderness of Sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And they took their journey out of the wilderness of Sin, and encamped in Dofkah &#91;Knocking&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And they departed from Dofkah &#91;Knocking&#93;, and encamped in Alush &#91;I Will Knead (Bread)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> And they removed from Alush &#91;I Will Knead (Bread)&#93;, and encamped at Refidim &#91;Resting Places&#93;, where was no water for the people to drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> And they departed from Refidim &#91;Resting Places&#93;, and pitched in the wilderness of Sinai.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And they removed from the desert of Sinai, and pitched at Kivrot-HaTa’avah &#91;Graves of Lust&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And they departed from Kivrot-HaTa’avah &#91;Graves of Lust&#93;, and encamped at Hatzerot &#91;A Place in the Wilderness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And they departed from Hatzerot &#91;A Place in the Wilderness&#93;, and pitched in Ritmah &#91;Health&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And they departed from Ritmah &#91;Health&#93;, and pitched at Rimmon &#91;Pomegranate&#93; -Peretz &#91;Pomegranate of the Breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And they departed from Rimmon &#91;Pomegranate&#93; -Peretz &#91;Pomegranate of the Breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet)&#93;, and pitched in Livnah &#91;Pavement&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> And they removed from Livnah &#91;Pavement&#93;, and pitched at Rissah &#91;Ruin&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And they journeyed from Rissah &#91;Ruin&#93;, and pitched in K’helah &#91;Assembly&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> And they went from K’helah &#91;Assembly&#93;, and pitched in mount Shefer &#91;Beauty&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> And they removed from mount Shefer &#91;Beauty&#93;, and encamped in Haradah &#91;Fear&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And they removed from Haradah &#91;Fear&#93;, and pitched in Mak’helot &#91;Place of Assembly&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And they removed from Mak’helot &#91;Place of Assembly&#93;, and encamped at Tachat &#91;Station&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And they departed from Tachat &#91;Station&#93;, and pitched at Terach &#91;Station&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And they removed from Terach &#91;Station&#93;, and pitched in Mitkah &#91;Sweetness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> And they went from Mitkah &#91;Sweetness&#93;, and pitched in HaShmonah &#91;Fatness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> And they departed from HaShmonah &#91;Fatness&#93;, and encamped at Moserot &#91;Bonds&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> And they departed from Moserot &#91;Bonds&#93;, and pitched in B’nei-Ya’akan &#91;Sons of Twisting&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> And they removed from B’nei-Ya’akan &#91;Sons of Twisting&#93;, and encamped at Hor-HaGidgad &#91;Cavern of Gidgad&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 26:37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Numbers 2:19"> And they went from Hor-HaGidgad &#91;Cavern of Gidgad&#93;, and pitched in Yotvatah &#91;Pleasantness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 26:28–34"> And they removed from Yotvatah &#91;Pleasantness&#93;, and encamped at Avronah &#91;Passage&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Numbers 26:34; Genesis 48:19–20; Numbers 2:21"> And they departed from Avronah &#91;Passage&#93;, and encamped at ‘Etzyon-Gever &#91;Backbone of a Man&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Genesis 49:27; Genesis 44:20; Revelation 7:8"> And they removed from ‘Etzyon-Gever &#91;Backbone of a Man&#93;, and pitched in the wilderness of Tzin &#91;Flat&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Numbers 26:41; Judges 20:44–46; 2 Chronicles 17:17"> And they removed from Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93;, and pitched in mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93;, in the edge of the land of Edom &#91;Red&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Genesis 46:23; Genesis 30:5–6; Genesis 49:16–17"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest went up into mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93; at the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and died there, in the fortieth year after the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; were come out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, in the first &#91;day&#93; of the fifth month.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Numbers 26:43; Numbers 2:26"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; &#91;was&#93; an hundred and twenty and three years old when he died in mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 30:12–13; Genesis 46:27; Genesis 49:20"> And king ‘Arad &#91;A Wild Ass&#93; the Kena’ani &#91;Merchants&#93;, which dwelt in the south in the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;, heard of the coming of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Numbers 26:47; Numbers 2:28"> And they departed from mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93;, and pitched in Tzalmonah &#91;Shady&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Genesis 49:21; Numbers 26:48–50; Genesis 46:24"> And they departed from Tzalmonah &#91;Shady&#93;, and pitched in Punon &#91;Darkness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="Numbers 26:50; Numbers 2:30"> And they departed from Punon &#91;Darkness&#93;, and pitched in Ovot &#91;Waterskins&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44" data-xref="Numbers 26:64; Numbers 1:2–16"> And they departed from Ovot &#91;Waterskins&#93;, and pitched in ‘Iyei-Ha’avarim &#91;Ruins of Regions Beyond&#93;, in the border of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And they departed from ‘Ee’yim &#91;Ruins&#93;, and pitched in Divon-Gad &#91;Wasting&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="Exodus 12:37; Numbers 26:51; Exodus 38:26"> And they removed from Divon-Gad &#91;Wasting&#93;, and encamped in ‘‘Almon &#91;concealed&#93; -Divlatayim &#91;Concealing the Two Cakes&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Numbers 2:33; Numbers 3:1–49; Numbers 26:57–62"> And they removed from ‘‘Almon &#91;concealed&#93; -Divlatayim &#91;Concealing the Two Cakes&#93;, and pitched in the mountains of ‘Avarim &#91;Regions Beyond&#93;, before Nevo &#91;Prophet&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And they departed from the mountains of ‘Avarim &#91;Regions Beyond&#93;, and pitched in the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; by Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; &#91;near&#93; Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49" data-xref="Numbers 26:62; Numbers 2:33"> And they pitched by Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, from Beit-HaYeshimot &#91;House of the Desolation&#93; &#91;even&#93; unto Avel-HaSheetim &#91;Meadow of Acacias&#93; in the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="Exodus 38:21; Exodus 31:18; Numbers 4:15"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; in the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; by Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; &#91;near&#93; Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51" data-xref="Numbers 3:38; Numbers 3:10; 1 Samuel 6:19"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, When you2f are passed over Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; into the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52" data-xref="Numbers 2:2; Numbers 2:34; Numbers 10:1–36"> Then you2f shall drive out all the inhabitants of the land from before you, and destroy all their pictures, and destroy all their molten images, and quite pluck down all their high places:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53" data-xref="Numbers 16:46; 1 Chronicles 23:32; Leviticus 10:6"> And you2f shall dispossess &#91;the inhabitants&#93; of the land, and dwell in it: for I have given you the land to possess it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54" data-xref="Deuteronomy 12:32; Exodus 40:16; Exodus 39:32"> And you2f shall divide the land by lot for an inheritance among your families: &#91;and&#93; to the more you2f shall give the more inheritance, and to the fewer you2f shall give the less inheritance: every man’s &#91;inheritance&#93; shall be in the place where his lot falls; according to the tribes of your fathers you2f shall inherit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> But if you2f will not drive out the inhabitants of the land from before you; then it shall come to pass, that those which you2f let remain of them &#91;shall be&#93; pricks in your eyes, and thorns in your sides, and shall vex you in the land wherein you2f dwell.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> Moreover it shall come to pass, &#91;that&#93; I shall do unto you, as I thought to do unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Command the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, When you2f come into the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;; (this &#91;is&#93; the land that shall fall unto you for an inheritance, &#91;even&#93; the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93; with the coasts thereof:)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> Then your south quarter shall be from the wilderness of Tzin &#91;Flat&#93; along by the coast of Edom &#91;Red&#93;, and your south border shall be the outmost coast of the salt sea eastward:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And your border shall turn from the south to the ascent of ‘Akrabbim &#91;Ascent of Scorpions&#93;, and pass on to Tzin &#91;Flat&#93;: and the going forth thereof shall be from the south to Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93; -Barnea &#91;Holy&#93;, and shall go on to Hatzar-Adar &#91;Enclosure of Glory&#93;, and pass on to ‘Atzmon &#91;Strong&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And the border shall fetch a compass (go around, encircle; encompass) from ‘Atzmon &#91;Strong&#93; unto the river of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and the goings out of it shall be at the sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And &#91;as for&#93; the western border, you2f shall even have the great sea for a border: this shall be your west border.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> And this shall be your north border: from the great sea you2f shall point out for you mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> From mount Hor &#91;Mountain Peak&#93; you2f shall point out &#91;your border&#93; unto the entrance of Hamat &#91;Fortress&#93;; and the goings forth of the border shall be to Tz’dad &#91;Mountain Side&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And the border shall go on to Zifon &#91;Fragrance&#93;, and the goings out of it shall be at Hatzar-’Einan &#91;Villiage of Fountains&#93;: this shall be your north border.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> And you2f shall point out your east border from Hatzar-’Einan &#91;Village of Fountains&#93; to Sh’fam &#91;Bald&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> And the coast shall go down from Sh’fam &#91;Bald&#93; to Rivlah &#91;Fertility&#93;, on the east side of ‘Ayin &#91;Spring&#93;; and the border shall descend, and shall reach unto the side of the sea of Kinneret &#91;Harps&#93; eastward:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And the border shall go down to Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, and the goings out of it shall be at the salt sea: this shall be your land with the coasts thereof round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; commanded the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, This &#91;is&#93; the land which you2f shall inherit by lot, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded to give unto the nine tribes, and to the half tribe:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> For the tribe of the children of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93; according to the house of their fathers, and the tribe of the children of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; according to the house of their fathers, have received &#91;their inheritance&#93;; and half the tribe of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; have received their inheritance:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> The two tribes and the half tribe have received their inheritance on this side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; &#91;near&#93; Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93; eastward, toward the sunrising.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> These &#91;are&#93; the names of the men which shall divide the land unto you: El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the priest, and Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; the son of Nun &#91;Posterity&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> And you2f shall take one prince of every tribe, to divide the land by inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> And the names of the men &#91;are&#93; these: Of the tribe of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, Kalev &#91;Dog&#93; the son of Y’funeh &#91;He Will Face&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> And of the tribe of the children of Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, Sh’mu’el &#91;His Name is El&#93; the son of Ammihud &#91;People of Magesty&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> Of the tribe of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, Elidad &#91;My God Has Loved&#93; the son of Kislon &#91;Confidence&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> And the prince of the tribe of the children of Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, Buki &#91;Wasting&#93; the son of Yogli &#91;He is Exiled&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> The prince of the children of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, for the tribe of the children of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, Hani’el &#91;Favoured of God&#93; the son of Efod &#91;A (Special) Belt (wrapped around)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> And the prince of the tribe of the children of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, K’mu’el &#91;Raised of God&#93; the son of Shiftan &#91;Judicial&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And the prince of the tribe of the children of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, Elitzafan &#91;My God has Protected&#93; the son of Parnakh &#91;Delicate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> And the prince of the tribe of the children of Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, Palti’el &#91;God Delivers&#93; the son of ‘Azan &#91;Very Strong&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And the prince of the tribe of the children of Asher &#91;Happy&#93;, Achihud &#91;Brother of Majesty&#93; the son of Shlomi &#91;Peaceful&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> And the prince of the tribe of the children of Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, P’dah’el &#91;Ransomed by God&#93; the son of Ammihud &#91;People of Magesty&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> These &#91;are they&#93; whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded to divide the inheritance unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; in the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; by Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; &#91;near&#93; Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> Command the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they give unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; of the inheritance of their possession cities to dwell in; and you2f shall give &#91;also&#93; unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; suburbs for the cities round about them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And the cities shall they have to dwell in; and the suburbs of them shall be for their cattle, and for their goods, and for all their beasts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And the suburbs of the cities, which you2f shall give unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, &#91;shall reach&#93; from the wall of the city and outward a thousand cubits round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And you2f shall measure from without the city on the east side two thousand cubits, and on the south side two thousand cubits, and on the west side two thousand cubits, and on the north side two thousand cubits; and the city &#91;shall be&#93; in the midst: this shall be to them the suburbs of the cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> And among the cities which you2f shall give unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;there shall be&#93; six cities for refuge, which you2f shall appoint for the manslayer, that he may flee there: and to them you2f shall add forty and two cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> &#91;So&#93; all the cities which you2f shall give to the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;shall be&#93; forty and eight cities: them &#91;shall you2f give&#93; with their suburbs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And the cities which you2f shall give &#91;shall be&#93; of the possession of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: from &#91;them that have&#93; many you2f shall give many; but from &#91;them that have&#93; few you2f shall give few: every one shall give of his cities unto the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; according to his inheritance which he inherits.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, When you2f be come over Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; into the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> Then you2f shall appoint you cities to be cities of refuge for you; that the slayer may flee there, which kills any person at unawares.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> And they shall be unto you cities for refuge from the avenger; that the manslayer die not, until he stand before the congregation in judgment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> And of these cities which you2f shall give six cities shall you2f have for refuge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Numbers 2:14; Numbers 7:42; Numbers 10:20"> You2f shall give three cities on this side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, and three cities shall you2f give in the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;, &#91;which&#93; shall be cities of refuge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Numbers 7:78; Numbers 10:27; Numbers 2:29"> These six cities shall be a refuge, &#91;both&#93; for the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and for the stranger, and for the sojourner among them: that every one that kills any person unawares may flee there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 7:2; Exodus 18:21; Exodus 18:25"> And if he strike him with an instrument of iron, so that he die, he &#91;is&#93; a murderer: the murderer shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="John 10:3; Revelation 7:4–17; Numbers 1:5–15"> And if he strike him with throwing a stone, which he may die, and he die, he &#91;is&#93; a murderer: the murderer shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezra 2:59; Nehemiah 7:61; Hebrews 7:3"> Or &#91;if&#93; he strike him with an hand weapon of wood, which he may die, and he die, he &#91;is&#93; a murderer: the murderer shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Numbers 1:2; Numbers 26:1–2; 2 Samuel 24:1–10"> The revenger of blood himself shall slay the murderer: when he meets him, he shall slay him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Numbers 26:5–7; Numbers 2:10–11; Genesis 46:9"> But if he thrust him of hatred, or hurl at him by laying of wait, that he die;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Numbers 26:7; Numbers 2:10–11"> Or in hatred (being an enemy) strike him with his hand, that he die: he that struck &#91;him&#93; shall surely be put to death; &#91;for&#93; he &#91;is&#93; a murderer: the revenger of blood shall slay the murderer, when he meets him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 26:12–14; Genesis 34:25–30; Numbers 2:12–13"> But if he thrust him suddenly without hatred (being an enemy), or have cast upon him anything without laying of wait,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 26:14; Numbers 2:13; Numbers 25:8–9"> Or with any stone, which a man may die, seeing &#91;him&#93; not, and cast &#91;it&#93; upon him, that he die, and &#91;was&#93; not his enemy, neither sought his harm:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 26:15–18; Genesis 46:16; Numbers 2:14–15"> Then the congregation shall judge between the slayer and the revenger of blood according to these judgments:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Numbers 26:18; Numbers 2:15"> And the congregation shall deliver the slayer out of the hand of the revenger of blood, and the congregation shall restore him to the city of his refuge, to which he was fled: and he shall abide in it unto the death of the High Priest, which was anointed with the holy oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Genesis 29:35; Numbers 26:19–22; 2 Samuel 24:9"> But if the slayer shall at any time come without the border of the city of his refuge, to which he was fled;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="2 Samuel 24:9; Numbers 26:22; 2 Chronicles 17:14–16"> And the revenger of blood find him without the borders of the city of his refuge, and the revenger of blood kill the slayer; he shall not be guilty of blood:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 46:13; Genesis 49:14–15; Genesis 30:18"> Because he should have remained in the city of his refuge until the death of the High Priest: but after the death of the High Priest the slayer shall return into the land of his possession.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Numbers 26:25; Numbers 2:6"> So these &#91;things&#93; shall be for a statute of judgment unto you throughout your generations in all your dwellings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Numbers 26:26–27; Genesis 49:13; Genesis 30:20"> Whoso kills any person, the murderer shall be put to death by the mouth of witnesses: but one witness shall not testify against any person &#91;to cause him&#93; to die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Numbers 26:27; Numbers 2:8"> Moreover you2f shall take no satisfaction for the life of a murderer, which &#91;is&#93; guilty of death: but he shall be surely put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Numbers 26:35–37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Genesis 46:20"> And you2f shall take no satisfaction for him that is fled to the city of his refuge, that he should come again to dwell in the land, until the death of the priest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 26:37; Deuteronomy 33:17; Numbers 2:19"> So you2f shall not pollute the land wherein you2f &#91;are&#93;: for blood it defiles the land: and the land cannot be cleansed of the blood that is shed in it, but by the blood of him that shed it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 26:28–34"> Defile not therefore the land which you2f shall inhabit, wherein I dwell: for I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) dwell among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Exodus 25:22; Exodus 19:1; Numbers 10:11–12"> And the chief fathers of the families of the children of Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;, the son of Makhir &#91;Sold&#93;, the son of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, of the families of the sons of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, came near, and spoke before Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and before the princes, the chief fathers of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Exodus 38:26; Exodus 30:12; Numbers 1:18"> And they said, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded my lord to give the land for an inheritance by lot to the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and my lord was commanded by the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to give the inheritance of Tz’lof’chad &#91;First-Born&#93; our brother unto his daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 30:14; Numbers 14:29; Numbers 33:1"> And if they be married to any of the sons of the &#91;other&#93; tribes of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, then shall their inheritance be taken from the inheritance of our fathers, and shall be put to the inheritance of the tribe whereunto they are received: so shall it be taken from the lot of our inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 1:16; Exodus 18:25; Numbers 13:2–15"> And when the jubilee of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall be, then shall their inheritance be put unto the inheritance of the tribe whereunto they are received: so shall their inheritance be taken away from the inheritance of the tribe of our fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Numbers 7:30; Genesis 30:5–20; Genesis 49:1–33"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; commanded the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying, The tribe of the sons of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; has said well.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Numbers 7:36; Numbers 2:12"> This &#91;is&#93; the thing which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) does command concerning the daughters of Tz’lof’chad &#91;First-Born&#93;, saying, Let them marry to whom they think best; only to the family of the tribe of their father shall they marry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Luke 3:32; Numbers 10:14; Gosel 1:2–5"> So shall not the inheritance of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; remove from tribe to tribe: for every one of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall keep himself to the inheritance of the tribe of his fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Numbers 7:18; Numbers 10:15; Numbers 2:5"> And every daughter, that possesses an inheritance in any tribe of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, shall be wife unto one of the family of the tribe of her father, that the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; may enjoy every man the inheritance of his fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Numbers 7:24; Numbers 10:16; Numbers 2:7"> Neither shall the inheritance remove from &#91;one&#93; tribe to another tribe; but every one of the tribes of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall keep himself to his own inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Numbers 7:54; Numbers 7:48; 1 Chronicles 7:26–27"> Even as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so did the daughters of Tz’lof’chad &#91;First-Born&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Numbers 7:60; Numbers 10:24; Numbers 2:22"> For Machlah &#91;Disease&#93;, Tirtzah &#91;Favorable&#93;, and Hoglah &#91;Partidge&#93;, and Machlah &#91;Queen&#93;, and No’ah &#91;Motion&#93;, the daughters of Tz’lof’chad &#91;First-Born&#93;, were married unto their father’s brothers’ sons:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Numbers 7:66; Numbers 2:25; Numbers 10:25"> &#91;And&#93; they were married into the families of the sons of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; the son of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, and their inheritance remained in the tribe of the family of their father.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Numbers 7:72; Numbers 10:26; Numbers 2:27"> These &#91;are&#93; the commandments and the judgments, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; by Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; &#91;near&#93; Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
